\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"-p1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--no-backup-if-mismatch\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"],\\\\\\\\n        input=diff_text, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=work_dir,\\\\\\\\n    )\\\\\\\\n proc.stderr[:500] if proc.stderr else \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n        }\\\\\\\\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\\\\\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"pass\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\":
